FOE Calendar

Steps and code for the FOE app. Dummy data only: nothing is saved and nothing is connected to a list yet.

1Create the app

  1. make.powerapps.com → Create → Blank canvas app. Name: FOE Calendar. Format: Tablet.
  2. Settings → Display: Landscape, 1366 x 768 or larger, Scale to fit on. Settings → Updates: modern controls on.
  3. No data connections yet.

2App.OnStart

Select App → OnStart, replace everything, paste. Then right-click App → Run OnStart.

App - OnStart v2 · 24/11/2026 09:11 GMT

ClearCollect(
    colEvents,
    ForAll(
        colBase As b,
        {
            Id: b.I,
            Title: b.T,
            StartDate: DateAdd(Today(), b.O, "Days"),
            EndDate: DateAdd(Today(), b.O + b.L - 1, "Days"),
            TypeKey: b.K,
            Status: If(b.S = "T", "To be confirmed", "Confirmed"),
            Kind: Coalesce(b.Kd, "event"),
            KeyDate: Coalesce(b.Kf, false),
            Overseas: Coalesce(b.Ov, false),
            Programme: Coalesce(b.Pg, "National Activity"),
            PeopleState: Coalesce(b.P, ""),
            PeopleNote: Coalesce(b.Pn, ""),
            MoneyState: Coalesce(b.M, ""),
            MoneyNote: Coalesce(b.Mn, ""),
            EquipState: Coalesce(b.E, ""),
            EquipNote: Coalesce(b.En, ""),
            BranchReq: Coalesce(b.B, false),
            BranchWho: Coalesce(b.W, ""),
            Location: "",
            Organiser: "",
            Notes: "",
            Sample: !IsBlank(b.P),
            Deleted: false,
            SortKey: b.O * 1000 - b.L
        }
    )
);
ClearCollect(colSelTypes, ForAll(Filter(colTypes, Active), {TKey: ThisRecord.TKey}));
ClearCollect(colScope, Filter(colEvents, !Deleted && (varProg = "all" || (varProg = "national" && Programme = "National Activity") || (varProg = "ctc" && Programme = "CTC") || (varProg = "ctt" && Programme = "CTTs") || (varProg = "overseas" && Overseas) || (varProg = "meet" && Kind <> "event"))));
ClearCollect(
    colVisible,
    AddColumns(
        SortByColumns(
            Filter(
                colScope,
                (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
            ),
            "SortKey",
            SortOrder.Ascending
        ),
        Concern,
        !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
        Hex,
        With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
    )
);
ClearCollect(
    colDays,
    ForAll(
        Sequence(42) As s,
        With(
            {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
            With(
                {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                With(
                    {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                    {
                        Idx: s.Value,
                        D: d,
                        N: CountRows(f),
                        T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                        F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                        B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                        W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                        S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                        I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                        T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                        F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                        B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                        W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                        S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                        I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                        T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                        F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                        B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                        W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                        S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                        I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                    }
                )
            )
        )
    )
);
ClearCollect(colList, Filter(colVisible, false))

Add a blank screen, paste, and delete any older scrCalendar or Screen1. It must end up named scrCalendar. Red errors on Navigate are expected until Steps 4 and 5 are done.

                        - btnARestore:
                            Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                            Properties:
                              BorderColor: =RGBA(208, 196, 204, 1)
                              BorderThickness: =1
                              Color: =RGBA(30, 24, 34, 1)
                              Fill: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                              FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                              Height: =32
                              HoverColor: =RGBA(30, 24, 34, 1)
                              HoverFill: =RGBA(240, 236, 240, 1)
                              OnSelect: |-
                                =Patch(colEvents, LookUp(colEvents, Id = ThisItem.Id), {Deleted: false});
                                ClearCollect(colScope, Filter(colEvents, !Deleted && (varProg = "all" || (varProg = "national" && Programme = "National Activity") || (varProg = "ctc" && Programme = "CTC") || (varProg = "ctt" && Programme = "CTTs") || (varProg = "overseas" && Overseas) || (varProg = "meet" && Kind <> "event"))));
                                ClearCollect(
                                    colVisible,
                                    AddColumns(
                                        SortByColumns(
                                            Filter(
                                                colScope,
                                                (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                                                (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                                                (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
                                            ),
                                            "SortKey",
                                            SortOrder.Ascending
                                        ),
                                        Concern,
                                        !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
                                        Hex,
                                        With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
                                    )
                                );
                                ClearCollect(
                                    colDays,
                                    ForAll(
                                        Sequence(42) As s,
                                        With(

                          ClearCollect(
                              colVisible,
                              AddColumns(
                                  SortByColumns(
                                      Filter(

                                          (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&

                                                  W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),

                                                  B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                  W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                  S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                                                  I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                                                  T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                                                  F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                  B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                  W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                  S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                                                  I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                                                  T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                                                  F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                  B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                  W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                  S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                                                  I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                                              }
                                          )
                                      )
                                  )
                              )
                          );
                          Set(varDlg, "detail");
                          Navigate(scrCalendar, ScreenTransition.Fade)
                        PressedFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 0.12)
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =10
                        RadiusBottomRight: =10
                        RadiusTopLeft: =10
                        RadiusTopRight: =10
                        Text: =""
                        Width: =Parent.TemplateWidth
                        X: =0
                        Y: =0

6Start screen

Select App, set StartScreen to scrHome, save, and run from Home.

If Studio reports an unknown property (PA2108), delete just that line and tell me which one.

7SharePoint lists (provisional)

Not connected to the app yet. Create them with New list → From CSV using the downloads, or by hand from the tables. The CSV import is untried. Do not load real data yet. Names must match exactly.

FOE Events

The events, meetings and conferences. One row per event. Download FOE_Events.csv

Column nameTypeChoices or notes
TitleSingle line of text (built in)Built-in. The event name.
StartDateDate and time (date only)Date only.
EndDateDate and time (date only)Date only. The same as the start for a one-day event.
ProgrammeChoiceChoices: National Activity, CTC, CTTs. Which programme owns the event.
KindChoiceChoices: event, meeting, conference. Lower case. Drives the Meetings and conferences tab.
TypeKeySingle line of textThe key of a row in FOE Event Types (for example exp, cer).
StatusChoiceChoices: Confirmed, To be confirmed.
KeyDateYes/NoShown on the home page.
OverseasYes/NoProvisional. Overseas is likely to become its own list.
PeopleStateChoiceChoices: needs, ok, none. Leave blank for "not yet set". The wording shown in the app is set in FOE Settings.
PeopleNoteMultiple lines of text (plain)What is needed.
MoneyStateChoiceChoices: needs, ok, none. As above.
MoneyNoteMultiple lines of text (plain)
EquipStateChoiceChoices: needs, ok, none. As above.
EquipNoteMultiple lines of text (plain)
BranchReqYes/NoSomeone from the branch needs to attend.
BranchWhoSingle line of textPlain text for now. Becomes a Person column once confirmed.
LocationSingle line of text
OrganiserSingle line of text
NotesMultiple lines of text (plain)Plain text. Do not record personal details here.
DeletedYes/NoSoft delete: admins can restore.

FOE Event Types

The event types, their colours and which programme they belong to. Admins edit this in the app. Download FOE_Event_Types.csv

Column nameTypeChoices or notes
TitleSingle line of text (built in)The name shown in the app (for example "Expeditions and adventurous").
TKeySingle line of textShort key, lower case, never changes (for example exp).
HexSingle line of textColour, for example #1F6B57.
ProgChoiceChoices: National Activity, CTC, CTTs. Which programme the type belongs to.
ActiveYes/NoHidden types disappear from forms but old events keep them.

FOE Settings

One row of app settings: the warning period and the wording of the states. Download FOE_Settings.csv

Column nameTypeChoices or notes
TitleSingle line of text (built in)Always "Settings". There is only one row.
WarnDaysNumberDays before an event that the warning appears (60).
LabelNeedsSingle line of textNeeds organising
LabelOkSingle line of textIn place
LabelNoReqSingle line of textNo requirement
LabelUnsetSingle line of textNot yet set
ResPeopleSingle line of textPeople
ResMoneySingle line of textMoney
ResEquipSingle line of textEquipment

FOE Users

Who is a viewer, editor or admin. Admins manage this in the app. Download FOE_Users.csv

Column nameTypeChoices or notes
TitleSingle line of text (built in)A label, for example the person's name and team.
PersonPerson or groupThe person, picked from the directory. Create this column by hand if the import does not.
RoleChoiceChoices: viewer, editor, admin. Lower case.
